问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 是 LP87702-Q1；VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：根据现有证据，无法完整回答“U1700的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络”。 **缺失信息**： 现有证据中，电路设计文件“825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF”仅确认U1700的型号为LP87702-Q1，但未提供其具体引脚的网络连接信息。文档文本中虽描述了U1700（LP87702）与MCU的控制关系（如MCU的P15.3引脚控制5V输出、P15.4/P15.5用于I2C通信、P12.1用于复位），但均是从MCU侧描述，未明确对应到U1700的特定引脚编号及其连接的网络。因此，**U1700每个关键引脚的网络连接信息缺失**，无法给出具体连接列表。 **证据冲突说明**： 证据中（来源：600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）存在多项字段取值冲突，例如： - 字段“SM Name”包含“fusa-2316-unused”、“fusa-2317-boundary”等多个不同值。 - 字段“SM TSR”包含“system”和“the”两个不同值。 - 字段“id”包含“eyeq4l_aoureq_2316”、“eyeq4l_aoureq_2317”等多个不同值。 **这些冲突与U1700的引脚-网络连接问题无关**，因此不影响本问题的回答结论，但已如实记录。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. 问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：基于检索到的证据，以下是对您问题的回答： ### 网络 VCC3V3 上连接的主要芯片 根据电路设计图和硬件接口文档，网络 VCC3V3 是一个系统级的电源网络，它为多个电源转换芯片和功能模块提供输入电压。主要连接的芯片及相关模块如下： 1. **电源转换与管理芯片 (直接连接 VCC3V3 网络)** * **U1500**: 根据电路图，其 `VIN` 引脚直接连接至 `VCC3V3` 网络。该芯片用于生成 `VCC3V3_ETH` 电源 [1]。 * **U1600**: 根据电路图，其 `VCCIN` 引脚直接连接至 `VCC3V3` 网络 [1]。 * **U1700**: 根据电路图，其多个电源引脚 (`VIN_B0_1`, `VIN_B1_1`, `VANA`, `VIN_B0_2`, `VIN_B1_2`) 直接连接至 `VCC3V3` 网络 [1]。 * **U1800**: 根据电路图，其 `VIN` 和 `MODE/SYNC` 引脚直接连接至 `VCC3V3` 网络 [1]。 * **U1802**: 根据电路图，其 `VIN` 引脚直接连接至 `VCC3V3` 网络 [1]。 * **U1804**: 根据电路图，其 `VIN` 引脚直接连接至 `VCC3V3` 网络 [1]。 * **U901**: 根据电路图，其 `S2` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_SW` (由 `VCC3V3` 通过开关 `Q1700` 控制) [7]。 2. **主控芯片 (通过分支网络连接)** * **U900C (MCU)**: 根据电路图，其 `VEVRSB`, `VEXT`, `VFLEX` 等引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_MCU` 网络，该网络是 `VCC3V3` 的一个分支 [6]。 * **U400-2 (EQ6)**: 根据电路图，其 `VDD_RING_8_0` 和 `VDD_RING_9_0` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_EQ` 网络，该网络是 `VCC3V3` 的一个分支 [4]。 * **U500 (晶振)**: 根据电路图，其 `VCC` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_EQ` 网络 [4]。 * **U1200, U1203, U1204, U1205, U1302, U1303, U1304, U1305 (DDR内存)**: 根据电路图，这些芯片的 `VI/O` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_MCU` 网络 [6]。 * **U503 (电平转换器)**: 根据电路图，其 `VCCB` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_MCU` 网络 [6]。 3. **其他功能模块芯片** * **U900A (传感器)**: 根据电路图，其 `AN12/EVADC_G1CH4/EDSADC_EDS0PB` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ` 网络，该网络是 `VCC3V3` 的一个分支 [2]。 * **U1501 (以太网相关)**: 根据电路图，其 `EN` 和 `IN` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_ETH` 网络，该网络是 `VCC3V3` 的一个分支 [5]。 * **Y500 (晶振)**: 根据电路图，其 `VCC` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_EQ` 网络 [4]。 * **Y1100 (晶振)**: 根据电路图，其 `VCC` 引脚连接至 `VCC3V3_MCU` 网络 [6]。 **总结：** 网络 `VCC3V3` 是系统的主电源干线，主要为各类电源转换芯片（如 LN10046, TPS22918, LP87702, SA23002, TPS62872 等）提供输入，并经过这些芯片转换为其他电压域后，供给主控芯片（MCU, EQ6）、内存、传感器、以太网控制器等模块。 ### 缺失信息说明 * 目前检索到的证据列出了连接到 `VCC3V3` 网络的引脚，但未明确给出所有连接芯片的完整型号名称。例如，U900 系列、U1600、U1700、U1800 等芯片的具体型号未在提供的电路图摘录中体现。 * `VCC3V3` 网络还连接了大量的电容、电阻、电感、测试点、连接器等无源器件和连接器，这些器件不属于“主要芯片”的范畴，因此未在列表中详细列出，但它们是该网络的重要组成部分 [1]。 ### 证据冲突说明 检索账本中标记了多项冲突，但与用户问题“网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片”无直接关联。这些冲突主要存在于文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 内部，涉及英文字段（如 `hw interface component`, `ecu connector`, `ob`）的取值不一致，例如 `hw interface component` 同时被标记为 `b` 和 `qm`。这些冲突不影响本问题的回答，但提醒用户该文档内部存在不一致的记录。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. 问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：好的，以下是根据您提供的证据，对您的问题进行的回答。 ### 子问题1：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗芯片？ **回答：** 给板子供电的主 PMIC 是 **LP87702-Q1**（具体型号为 LP87702RRHBRQ1）。 **来源说明：** * 电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中，实例 `U1700` 的型号被明确标注为 `LP87702-Q1`，封装为 `pqfn50p5x5x90-32`。 * 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中也多次提及 `LP87702-Q1` 器件及其工作模式。 ### 子问题2：该主 PMIC 输出哪些电压轨？ **缺失信息：** **证据中未明确列出该主 PMIC 输出的具体电压值列表。** **详细说明：** 当前证据仅能确认该 PMIC 模块（`Group 17 · Power/PMIC` 和 `Group 15 · Power/PMIC`）与板上的其他电源模块（如 `Group 6 · Power/PMIC`、`Group 18 · Power/PMIC`）存在连接，并且通过一些网络（如 `GND`、`MCU_SCL_P15.4`、`MCU_SDA_P15.5`）进行通信。然而，这些证据**没有提供**该 PMIC 的各个输出引脚（如 VOUT1、VOUT2 等）的具体电压值。因此，无法从现有证据中回答“它输出哪些电压”这一问题。 ### 子问题3：软件如何配置该主 PMIC？ **缺失信息：** **证据中未提供完整的软件配置方法（如初始化序列、驱动代码示例等）。** **详细说明：** 现有证据仅提供了部分寄存器信息，这些信息不足以构成完整的软件配置方法。 1. **寄存器信息：** 文档 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 提到了该 PMIC 的寄存器映射，例如 `CONTROL1`（地址 1h，复位值 2Ah），`CONTROL2`（地址 2h，复位值 9h），`CONTROL3`（地址 3h，复位值 0h），`STATUS`（地址 4h，复位值 2h）。 2. **配置描述：** 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中提到了一个寄存器 `FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_REACTION1` 及其相关位域（如 `VMON_LDO1_OV_FS_REACTION` 和 `VMON_LDO1_UV_FS_REACTION`）的描述，描述了不同配置值对 RSTB 和 FS0B 引脚的影响。 **结论：** 虽然证据表明软件配置是通过 I2C 接口（证据中提到了 `MCU_SCL_P15.4` 和 `MCU_SDA_P15.5` 网络）修改这些寄存器来实现的，但**缺少**一个完整的、系统的配置指南或驱动代码来展示如何从初始化到运行时对 PMIC 进行配置。 ### 证据冲突说明 根据 `coverage_matrix.conflicts`，证据中存在以下冲突，**无法合并为确定结论**： * **地址 (address) 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `address` 字段给出了 `1h`, `2h`, `3h`, `4h` 等多个不同取值。 * **复位 (reset) 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `reset` 字段给出了 `0h`, `2ah`, `2h`, `9h` 等多个不同取值。 * **寄存器 (register) 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `register` 字段给出了 `fs_i_ovuv_safe_`, `nan`, `register` 等多个不同取值。 * **符号 (symbol) 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `symbol` 字段给出了 `nan`, `symbol`, `vmon_ldo1_ov_`, `vmon_ldo1_uv_` 等多个不同取值。 * **描述 (description) 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `description` 字段给出了 `00`, `01 v`, `10 v`, `11 v`, `description`, `nan`, `reset` 等多个不同取值。 * **寄存器.1 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `register.1` 字段给出了 `nan` 和 `note` 两个不同取值。 * **描述.1 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `description.1` 字段给出了 `111`, `nan`, `reset` 等多个不同取值。 * **频率 (fsw) 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `fsw` 字段给出了 `1.5`, `2.25`, `2.5`, `3` 等多个不同取值。 * **CAN 收发器型号 (tcan1044a) 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `tcan1044a` 字段给出了 `1` 和 `tcan1145` 两个不同取值。 * **MC (mc) 字段冲突：** 不同证据对 `mc` 字段给出了 `001`, `100`, `111` 等多个不同取值。 **注意：** 这些冲突主要集中在寄存器映射的详细描述上，表明不同来源的文档对同一寄存器或字段的描述可能存在不一致，因此在配置软件时需要特别注意，不能直接使用冲突值。

This register returns the device status flags. 10 Application and Implementation Note Information in the following applications sections is not part of the Tl component specification, and Tl does not warrant its accuracy or completeness. Tl's customers are responsible for determining suitability of components for their purposes, as well as validating and testing their design implementation to confirm systemfunctionality. 10.1 Application Information The following section discusses selection of the external components to complete the power supply design for typical a application. 10.2 Typical Application 10.2.1 Design Requirements Preliminary Calculations The maximum allowable deviation of the power supply is ±3.3%. The DC accuracy of the TPS6287x-Q1 is specified as ±1%, therefore, the maximum output voltage variation during a transient is given by: VoUT = ± VouT × (3.3% - 1%) = ±17.25 mV 10.2.2 Detailed Design Procedure The following subsections describe how to calculate the external components required to meet the specified transient requirements of a given application. The calculations include the worst-case variation of components and use the RMS method to combine the variation of uncorrelated parameters. 10.2.2.1 Selecting the Inductor The TPS6287x-Q1 devices have been optimized for inductors in the range 50 nH to 300 nH. If the transient response of the converter is limited by the slew rate of the current in the inductor, using a smaller inductor can improve performance. However, the output ripple current increases as the value of the inductor decreases and higher output current ripple generates higher output voltage ripple, which adds to the transient overshoot or undershoot. The optimal configuration for a given application is always a trade-off between a number of parameters. Tl recommends a starting value of 110 nH for typical applications. | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | If the junction temperature falls below the thermal shutdown threshold of 150°C (typical), the device: · Starts switching again, starting with a new soft-start sequence · Sets the EN pin to high impedance ·Sets the PG pin to high-impedance The device clears the TSHUT bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TSHUT threshold of 150°C (typical). In a stacked configuration, in which all devices share a common enable signal, a thermal shutdown condition in one device disables the entire stack. When the hot device cools down, the whole stack automatically starts switching again. 9.3.17 Stacked Operation The user can connect multiple devices in parallel in what is known as a "stack"; for example, to increase output current capability or reduce device junction temperature. A stack comprises one primary device and one or more secondary devices. During initialization, each device monitors its SYNC_OUT pin to determine if must operate as a primary device or a secondary device: · If there is a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground, the device operates as a secondary device. · If the SYNC_OUT pin is high impedance, the device operates as a primary device. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The key points to note are: All the devices in the stack share a common enable signal, which must be pulled up with a resistance of at least 15 kQ. All the devices in the stack share a common power-good signal. All the devices in the stack share a common compensation signal. All secondary devices must connect a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground. The remote sense pins (VOSNS and GOSNS) of each device must be connected (do not leave these pins floating). Each device must be configured for the same switching frequency.· The primary device must be configured for forced PwM operation (secondary devices are automatically configured for forcedPwM operation). A stacked configuration can support synchronization to an external clock or spread-spectrum clocking.· Only the VSEL pin of the primary device is used to set the default output voltage. The VSEL pin of secondary· devices is not used and must be connected to ground. The SDA and SCL pins of secondary devices are not used and must be connected to ground. | If the user applies a valid clock signal to the MODE/SYNC pin, the device synchronizes its switching cycles to the external clock and automatically selects forced PwM operation. The MODE/SYNC pin is logically ORed with the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Table 9-1). When multiple devices are used together in a stacked configuration, the MODE/SYNC pin of the secondary devices is the input for the clock signal (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.9 Spread Spectrum Clocking (SSC) The device has a spread spectrum clocking function that can reduce electromagnetic interference (EMl). When the SSC function is active, the device modulates the switching frequency to approximately ±10% the nominal value. The frequency modulation has a triangular characteristic (see Figure 9-11). To use the SSC function, make sure that: · SSCEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. · Forced PWM operation is selected (MODE pin is high or FPWMEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register). Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 ·The device is not synchronized to an external clock. To disable the SSC function, ensure that SCCEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. To use the SSC function with multiple devices in a stacked configuration, ensure that the primary converter runs from its internal oscillator and synchronize all secondary converters to the primary clock (see Figure 9-14). 9.3.10 Output Discharge The device has an output discharge function that ensures a defined ramp down of the output voltage when the device is disabled and keeps the output voltage close to 0 V while the device is off. The output discharge function is enabled when DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The output discharge function is enabled by default. If enabled, the device discharges the output under the following conditions: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = O in the CONTROL1 register. · A thermal shutdown event occurs. · A UVLO event occurs. · An OVLO event occurs. The output discharge function is not available until the user has enabled the device at least once after power up During power down, the device continues to discharge the output for as long as the internal supply voltage is greater than approximately 1.8 V. | window comparator monitors the output voltage, and the output of the comparator goes high if the output voltage is either less than 95% (typical) or greater than 105% (typical) of the nominal output voltage. The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | Register：nan; Symbol：nan; Description：nan; Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：nan; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：111 Closed Window : 50 % / Open Window : 50 % ——13_Registers Bit Description Register：Register; Symbol：Symbol; Description：Description; Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：nan; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：Reset on Power On Reset (POR) ——13_Registers Bit Description Register：FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_ REACTION1; Symbol：VMON_LDO1_OV_ FS_REACTION; Description：00 No effect on RSTB and FS0B; Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：nan; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：nan ——13_Registers Bit Description Register：nan; Symbol：nan; Description：01 VMON_LDO1 OV asserts FS0B only; Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：nan; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：nan ——13_Registers Bit Description Register：nan; Symbol：nan; Description：10 VMON_LDO1 OV asserts RSTB and FS0B; Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：nan; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：nan ——13_Registers Bit Description Register：nan; Symbol：nan; Description：11 VMON_LDO1 OV asserts RSTB and FS0B; Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：Note:本页均为配置寄存器，以支持需求开发人员快速确定具体项目需要配置的bits; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：nan ——13_Registers Bit Description Register：nan; Symbol：nan; Description：Reset on Power On Reset (POR); Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：nan; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：nan ——13_Registers Bit Description Register：nan; Symbol：VMON_LDO1_UV_ FS_REACTION; Description：00 No effect on RSTB and FS0B; Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：nan; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：nan ——13_Registers Bit Description Register：nan; Symbol：nan; Description：01 VMON_LDO1 OV asserts FS0B only; Unnamed: 3：nan; Register.1：nan; Symbol.1：nan; Description.1：nan ——13_Registers Bit Description | Figure 9-14. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. 问题：0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，针对您的问题“0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？”，回答如下： ### 1. 0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生？ **答案：** 由芯片 **TPS62872** 产生，具体型号为 **TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1**（器件实例 U1800）。 **证据来源：** * **电路设计文件** (825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF)：实例 U1800 的型号为 TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1，其 VOSNS 引脚连接到网络 VCC0V75，该网络为多个器件提供 0.75V 电源。 * **设计文档** (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx)：明确指出“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872”。 ### 2. 该芯片的输入是什么？ **答案：** 输入电压是 **VCC3V3**（3.3V）。 **证据来源：** * **设计文档** (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx)：在说明 TPS62872 的功能时，明确指出“VCC3V3转VCC0V75”，并在模块方案描述中再次确认“采用TPS62872电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75”。 ### 3. 文档有什么配置要求？ **答案：** 文档中对该电源的配置要求如下： 1. **使能控制**：由 MCU 的 **P22.0** 引脚控制该芯片的使能。 2. **输出电压设置与通信**：通过 MCU 的 **I2C** 接口进行设置和通信，具体引脚为 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 和 **MCU_SCL_P15.4**。 3. **寄存器配置**：文档提到存在“TPS62872配置”章节，并指出“TPS62872运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器”，但**未提供具体的寄存器配置表或配置值**。 **证据来源：** * **设计文档** (600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx)： * 模块方案描述：“用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。” * 文档结构描述：文档中包含“**TPS62872配置**”章节，并说明“TPS62872运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-3 TPS62872 Configuration”。但该表格的具体内容在提供的证据中缺失。 ### 缺失信息说明 * **具体的寄存器配置内容**：设计文档中提到的 “Table 5-3 TPS62872 Configuration” 的具体内容（如寄存器地址、值、配置目的等）在提供的证据中未呈现，因此无法提供具体的芯片配置参数。 ### 证据冲突说明 在提供的证据中，存在来自同一文档（`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）的HWFMEA表格数据，其内容涉及多个不同的功能模块（如 `HWC_1.18_DEBUG`, `HWC_1.3_VLDO1`, `HWC_1.4_VLDO2` 等），这些模块对应的“function”、“requirement”、“potential failure mode”等字段取值不同，但这些冲突**均不相关**于您所询问的0.75V电源（TPS62872芯片）的配置信息。这些冲突主要描述了系统基础芯片（SBC）FS26的其它功能模块的失效模式和安全机制，不影响上述关于0.75V电源的结论。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | OTHERQUALIFIEDVERSIONSOFTPS62872-Q1,TPS62873-Q1: ·Catalog :TPS62872,TPS62873 NOTE: Qualified Version Definitions: ·Catalog - Tl's standard catalog product TAPEANDREELINFORMATION PACKAGE MATERIALSINFORMATION PLASTIC QUAD FLAT PACK- NO LEAD NOTES: All linear dimensions are in millimeters. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table>

问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节声明存在 3V3 过压保护电路；EDF 只能用于说明观察到的二极管或 TVS 连接拓扑，不能在没有数据手册证据时断言具体阈值或保护能力。
实际回答：根据检索到的证据，以下是关于3.3V电源过压保护的具体回答： ### 1. 3.3V电源是否有过压保护？ **有。** 针对电源网络`VCC3V3_MCU`（即3V3_MCU），设计有硬件过压保护电路。 ### 2. 是如何实现的？ 该过压保护电路由**外部硬件电路**实现，具体机制如下： - **保护阈值**：当`VCC3V3`电压超过 **4.03V** 时，触发过压保护。 - **实现方式**：电路通过一个带cathode引脚的器件（可能为比较器或基准源）控制一个NMOS和一个PMOS的开关状态。 - **正常状态（VCC3V3 < 4.03V）**：cathode引脚为高阻态，NMOS和PMOS导通，`VCC3V3_SW`（输出给后级负载的电源）与`VCC3V3`直通，正常供电。 - **过压状态（VCC3V3 > 4.03V）**：cathode引脚输出逻辑低电平（最高1V），NMOS关闭，PMOS关闭，从而切断`VCC3V3_SW`的供电，实现对后级负载的过压保护。 **来源**：文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`（证据ID: `document:6620a6e887eb13f5`）中的表格明确描述了该方案。 ### 缺失信息 1. **保护范围限定**：现有证据仅明确描述了针对`VCC3V3_MCU`这一路电源的过压保护。其他3.3V电源网络（如`VCC3V3_EQ`、`VCC3V3_ETH`、`VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ`等）是否具备类似的过压保护机制 **未说明**。电路设计文件（`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）中未显示这些网络有类似的保护电路。 2. **具体器件型号**：文档中未给出实现该保护功能的比较器、基准源、MOS管等具体元器件的型号或Part Number，因此无法从Datasheet层面确认其保护能力。 3. **电路拓扑细节**：电路设计文件（`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）中虽然列出了`VCC3V3_MCU`网络的连接，但未提供该过压保护电路的完整拓扑连接关系（如哪个器件是负责比较的，哪个是开关管）。文档中描述的原理图（Scheme）未在证据中提供。 ### 证据冲突说明 检索到的证据元数据中存在冲突，但**这些冲突与“3.3V电源过压保护”的核心问题无关**。冲突主要涉及同一文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`中其他不同表格或字段的取值（如`accuracy`、`no.`、`alarm id`、`sri`、`alarm name`等），这些字段的值在不同条目中不一致。这些冲突不影响本问题的结论。 ### 补充说明：关于TVS管的保护 电路设计文件中检测到多个TVS二极管（如D1200、D1201、D1300、D1400、D1601等），但**这些TVS管均连接在CAN总线、以太网接口或UBD等信号/电源线上，并非直接连接在3.3V电源主路上**。因此，它们不构成对3.3V电源的过压保护，而是用于保护其连接的总线或接口免受ESD（静电放电）或浪涌电压的冲击。

When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. 问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，板上共使用了3个TPS22918电源开关，分别是U1500、U1802和U1803。以下逐个列出其输入、输出和使能（EN）引脚连接情况： --- ### 1. U1500 (TPS22918) - **输入（VIN）**：证据中未提供U1500的VIN连接网络，无法确定。 - **输出（VOUT）**：证据中未提供U1500的VOUT连接网络，无法确定。 - **使能（EN/ON）**：连接到网络 **MCU_3V3_ETH_EN**（证据8）。 ### 2. U1802 (TPS22918) - **输入（VIN）**：证据中未提供U1802的VIN连接网络，无法确定。 - **输出（VOUT）**：证据中未提供U1802的VOUT连接网络，无法确定。 - **使能（EN/ON）**：连接到网络 **MCU_3V3_EQ_EN**（证据7）。 ### 3. U1803 (TPS22918) - **输入（VIN）**：证据中未提供U1803的VIN连接网络，无法确定。 - **输出（VOUT）**：证据中未提供U1803的VOUT连接网络，无法确定。 - **使能（EN/ON）**：连接到网络 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**（证据6）。 --- ### 缺失信息 - 每个实例的**输入（VIN）** 和**输出（VOUT）** 网络在提供的电路设计证据中**缺失**，无法从现有数据中确定。 - 设计文档中可能包含更详细的引脚连接信息，但当前检索未返回这些内容，属于证据缺口。 ### 证据冲突 检索到的证据冲突（conflicts）涉及其他芯片（TPS6287x-Q1）的开关频率和打嗝使能参数，与TPS22918无关，此处不适用。 ### 说明 以上信息完全基于证据中的电路实例和网络连接，未做任何推断。如需完整电源开关电路，请补充电路图中引脚级连接数据。

Connect to GND for secondary devices in stacked operation and for device variants without I2C.</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >I2C serial clock pin. Do not leave this pin floating. Connect a pullup resistor to a logic high level.</td></tr> <tr><td >SCL</td><td >13</td><td >1/0</td><td >Connect this pin to GND for secondary devices in stacked operation and for device variants</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >without I2C.</td></tr> </table> | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. The SSTIME[1:0] bits in the CONTROL2 register select the duration of the soft-start ramp: | <table><caption> over operating junction temperature (Tj = -40 °℃ to 150 °C) and Vin = 2.7 V to 6 V. If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. Table 9-7 summarizes the behavior of the PG pin in a standalone or primary device. | If the user applies a valid clock signal to the MODE/SYNC pin, the device synchronizes its switching cycles to the external clock and automatically selects forced PwM operation. The MODE/SYNC pin is logically ORed with the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Table 9-1). When multiple devices are used together in a stacked configuration, the MODE/SYNC pin of the secondary devices is the input for the clock signal (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.9 Spread Spectrum Clocking (SSC) The device has a spread spectrum clocking function that can reduce electromagnetic interference (EMl). When the SSC function is active, the device modulates the switching frequency to approximately ±10% the nominal value. The frequency modulation has a triangular characteristic (see Figure 9-11). To use the SSC function, make sure that: · SSCEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. · Forced PWM operation is selected (MODE pin is high or FPWMEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register). Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 ·The device is not synchronized to an external clock. To disable the SSC function, ensure that SCCEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. To use the SSC function with multiple devices in a stacked configuration, ensure that the primary converter runs from its internal oscillator and synchronize all secondary converters to the primary clock (see Figure 9-14). 9.3.10 Output Discharge The device has an output discharge function that ensures a defined ramp down of the output voltage when the device is disabled and keeps the output voltage close to 0 V while the device is off. The output discharge function is enabled when DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The output discharge function is enabled by default. If enabled, the device discharges the output under the following conditions: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = O in the CONTROL1 register. · A thermal shutdown event occurs. · A UVLO event occurs. · An OVLO event occurs. The output discharge function is not available until the user has enabled the device at least once after power up During power down, the device continues to discharge the output for as long as the internal supply voltage is greater than approximately 1.8 V. | If the junction temperature falls below the thermal shutdown threshold of 150°C (typical), the device: · Starts switching again, starting with a new soft-start sequence · Sets the EN pin to high impedance ·Sets the PG pin to high-impedance The device clears the TSHUT bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TSHUT threshold of 150°C (typical). In a stacked configuration, in which all devices share a common enable signal, a thermal shutdown condition in one device disables the entire stack. When the hot device cools down, the whole stack automatically starts switching again. 9.3.17 Stacked Operation The user can connect multiple devices in parallel in what is known as a "stack"; for example, to increase output current capability or reduce device junction temperature. A stack comprises one primary device and one or more secondary devices. During initialization, each device monitors its SYNC_OUT pin to determine if must operate as a primary device or a secondary device: · If there is a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground, the device operates as a secondary device. · If the SYNC_OUT pin is high impedance, the device operates as a primary device. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The key points to note are: All the devices in the stack share a common enable signal, which must be pulled up with a resistance of at least 15 kQ. All the devices in the stack share a common power-good signal. All the devices in the stack share a common compensation signal. All secondary devices must connect a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground. The remote sense pins (VOSNS and GOSNS) of each device must be connected (do not leave these pins floating). Each device must be configured for the same switching frequency.· The primary device must be configured for forced PwM operation (secondary devices are automatically configured for forcedPwM operation). A stacked configuration can support synchronization to an external clock or spread-spectrum clocking.· Only the VSEL pin of the primary device is used to set the default output voltage. The VSEL pin of secondary· devices is not used and must be connected to ground. The SDA and SCL pins of secondary devices are not used and must be connected to ground. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F19</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>1V8转1V8_EQ用于EQ6供电，现选择负载开关方案。 1V8 to 1V8_EQ is used for the EQ6 power supply, now choose the load switch scheme. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ，给EQ6供电。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch chip is used to convert the VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ to power EQ6. 问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据现有证据，无法完整回答以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源的逐级供电链路。以下按子问题组织说明，并列出缺失信息和证据冲突。 ### 子问题1：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源引脚直接连接哪个电源节点？ **状态**：covered（但证据不充分） - **支持证据**：原理图（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中识别到 PHY 芯片 U1400（DP83TC812R-Q1），但未在其引脚连接中直接发现 1.0V 电源网络（如 VDDIO 或 VDD 相关的 net）。文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中提到了“VCC1V0_ETH”电源电压和对应的 ADC 采样信号“1V0_ETH_ACQ”，但未说明该电源节点直接连接的器件（如 LDO 或 DC-DC 输出）。 - **缺失信息**：原理图中以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源引脚（如 VDD、VDDIO 等）具体连接的网络名称，以及该网络直接连接的电源转换器（LDO/DC-DC）输出引脚。 ### 子问题2：该电源节点的输入电源来自哪里？请逐级向上追溯直到主电源输入。 **状态**：covered（但证据严重不足） - **支持证据**：文档（同上）中描述了电源管理单元 LP87702 实现“VCC3V3 转 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”三路输出，但未提及 1.0V 电源。也没有任何证据表明 1.0V 电源来自 LP87702 或其他电源芯片。此外，文档中“VCC1V0_ETH”仅用于 ADC 采样，未提供供电链路信息。 - **缺失信息**： - 1.0V 电源节点的具体输入来源（如 LDO 的输入电压、DC-DC 的输入电压）。 - 任何中间电源转换级（如从 3.3V 或 5V 降压到 1.0V 的器件）。 - 主电源输入（如 12V 或电池电压）到该电源节点的完整链路。 ### 证据冲突 检索账本中存在以下冲突（不同证据对同一字段给出了不同取值）： - **字段**：`hw interface component` 取值有 `b` 和 `qm`，这是由于文档中不同电源采样通道（如 EQ 系统和 QM 系统）对应的组件不同，但并非针对 1.0V 电源本身，不影响结论。 - **字段**：`value` 列表中包含大量电源轨名称（如 `1v0_eth_acq`、`1v25`、`1v8` 等），这些是不同电源网络的标识，并非冲突，而是不同电源节点的区分。 ### 综合结论 **无法追溯以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源的供电链路。** 现有证据仅能确认存在“VCC1V0_ETH”这个电源节点，但其直接连接、输入来源和逐级上级电源均未在原理图或文档中明确给出。要完整回答，需补充： - 原理图中 PHY 芯片 1.0V 电源引脚的网络连接。 - 电源树文档或电源芯片的详细连接图，说明该 1.0V 电源是由哪个转换器（如 LDO、DC-DC）从哪一级输入电压产生。

None：HI-Z，硬件外部下拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F24</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>正常模式、复位模式，待机模式，断电模式，休眠模式 Normal mode, reset mode, standby mode, power off mode, sleep mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 正常模式 模块上电，正常运行模式，以太网收发正常。MDIO可以访问，Link处于连接状态。 2、 复位模式 UV或Reset event时，PHY芯片将进行复位，在Reset期间将无法LINKUP，MDIO无法总线访问 待机模式 仅当PHY为MASTER且配置成管理模式时，上电自动进入待机模式。如果是自动模式，上电后自动进入正常模式。 4、 断电模式 当任何一个电源轨低于POR阈值（0.6V）时，PHY处于断电状态，所有数字IO都将保持高阻态。 休眠模式 当有休眠指令且MDI总线空闲时，将进入休眠模式，除了能量检测外，禁用所有PHY模块。在睡眠模式下，所有寄存器配置都会丢失。 Normal mode Power on，Normal Run mode, Ethernet transceiver is normal. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 上电模式 系统上电经过EMI电路为系统一级电源供电。 2、 下电模式 系统下电。 3、 运行模式 系统正常供电。 1. Power on mode: System power on through the EMI circuit for the system level power supply. 2. Power down mode.:Power down system. 3. Run mode：The system is powered normally.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td>无软件控制接口</td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>NA </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>NA</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>KL30 EMI电路为系统供电滤波电路，不涉及软件控制逻辑。 The KL30 EMI circuit powers the filter circuit for the system and does not involve software control logic.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F17</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH is used for ETH power supply, and the load switching scheme is now selected.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_ETH，提供给MCU的ETH供电。P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch is used to convert the VCC3V3 system power to VCC3V3-ETH to supply power to ETH of the MCU. The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：A5; None：P10.2; None：P10.2/GTM_TIM4_IN5_12/GTM_TIM1_IN2_3/CAN02_RXDE/QSPI1_SCLKA/QSPI1_SCLK/EVADC_FC3BFLOUT; None：QSPI1_SCLK; None：MMCU_SPI1_CLK_P10.2; None：/; None：O; None：SPI1 MCU透过电平转换芯片至FLASH（EQ6L）的时钟信号; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. The SSTIME[1:0] bits in the CONTROL2 register select the duration of the soft-start ramp: | <table><caption> (8) (9) (10) Table 10-3 lists a number of capacitors suitable for this application. 问题：U1700 的替代料和采购单价是多少？
参考答案：当前知识库只有电路和 HSI 文档，没有 BOM、替代料或价格表证据；只能确认 U1700 的已知料号为 20200424007，不能编造替代料或单价。
实际回答：根据现有证据，对您的问题回答如下： ## 1. U1700 的替代料有哪些？ **结论：无法确定。** 证据[1]（电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）显示 U1700 的器件型号为 **LP87702-Q1**，具体料号为 **LP87702RRHBRQ1**，封装为 `pqfn50p5x5x90-32`。 但所有已检索的证据（包括文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 和 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`）中**均未提及该器件的任何替代料**。 **缺口说明**：缺少明确列出 U1700 替代料的文档或表格（如 BOM 表、替代料清单等）。 ## 2. U1700 及其替代料的采购单价是多少？ **结论：无法确定。** 所有已检索的证据（包括电路设计、HSI 文档、datasheet）中**均未包含任何采购单价信息**。 **缺口说明**：缺少包含 U1700 及其替代料价格的采购清单、报价单或 BOM 成本表等证据。 ## 证据冲突说明 此问题相关证据中未发现冲突。证据覆盖度中列出的冲突（如“ecu connector”字段、“hw interface component”字段等）均与 U1700 的替代料和单价无关，不影响本问题结论。

问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：根据检索到的证据，主控MCU的型号已明确，具体信息如下： **主控MCU的型号**：**SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA** - **来源说明**： - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的模块功能描述指出：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”（证据[10]）。 - 同一文档的模块表格中，F01模块（MCU）的功能定义明确为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”（证据[13]）。 - 其他文档段落（如证据[9]、[14]、[15]、[16]）虽涉及MCU相关引脚或电源描述，但未提供型号，仅作为补充佐证。 - **缺失信息**：无。该子问题在检索账本中状态为“covered”，证据充分。 **证据冲突说明**： 在整体证据中，存在关于电压值、引脚网络名称等无关字段的冲突（如电压值1.2-1.35V与3.3V、多组引脚编号等），但这些冲突均不涉及主控MCU型号，因此不影响本问题的结论。已明确型号的唯一性。

Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：好的，请参考以下基于给定证据的分析。 ### 关于U1800的输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络 根据提供的证据，U1800 (TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1) 是一个电源转换芯片（VCC3V3转VCC0V75）。然而，关于您提出的四个方面，现有证据只能提供部分信息，具体如下： #### 1. 输入引脚 * **证据**: 电路设计图显示，U1800的`SCL`引脚连接到网络`MCU_SCL_P15.4`，SDA引脚连接到网络`MCU_SDA_P15.5`[3][4]。此外，U1800的型号为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1，这是一款电源转换芯片[5]。 * **结论**: 证据仅能确认U1800的I²C通信引脚（SCL和SDA）的连接，但未提供其**电源输入引脚**（如VIN）或其他功能输入引脚的具体连接信息。 * **缺失信息**: * U1800的具体电源输入引脚（如VIN）及其连接网络。 * 其他功能输入引脚（如EN、VSEL等）的连接网络。 #### 2. 输出检测 * **证据**: 在文档中，关于U1800的部分提到“TPS62872配置 TPS62872 Configuration”以及“TPS62872运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-3 TPS62872 Configuration”[14]。这表明MCU可以通过I²C读取U1800的状态。 * **结论**: U1800支持通过I²C接口进行输出检测（如读取电压、状态等），但现有证据**未提供**具体的输出检测功能（如检测什么参数、如何工作）的详细描述。 * **缺失信息**: * 输出检测的具体功能，例如是否检测输出电压、过流、过温等。 * 输出检测的工作机制，例如如何通过I²C读取状态寄存器，以及相应的寄存器地址和定义。 #### 3. 使能控制机制 * **证据**: 文档中[16]提到：“电源转换芯片TPS62872，VCC3V3转VCC0V75。TPS62872由MCU进行使能和关断控制。” 并且描述了MCU的引脚`P22.0`输出信号`MCU_VCC0V75_EN`，高电平使能，低电平关断。 * **结论**: U1800的使能控制是由MCU通过一个名为`MCU_VCC0V75_EN`的GPIO信号控制的，该信号连接到U1800的使能引脚（EN）。控制逻辑为：高电平使能，低电平关断。 * **缺失信息**: * U1800的具体使能引脚（EN）在电路图中的连接位置。 * 使能引脚的详细电气特性（如阈值电压、上拉/下拉情况）。 #### 4. I²C控制网络 * **证据**: * 电路设计图显示，U1800的`SCL`引脚连接到网络`MCU_SCL_P15.4`，`SDA`引脚连接到网络`MCU_SDA_P15.5`[3][4]。 * 这两个网络同时连接到MCU（U900E）的`P15.4`和`P15.5`引脚，分别对应`I2C0_SCL`和`I2C0_SDA`功能[3][4]。 * 数据手册片段显示，TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1的I²C地址为`0x41`，启动电压为0.875V [19]。 * **结论**: U1800的I²C控制网络通过`MCU_SCL_P15.4`（I2C0_SCL）和`MCU_SDA_P15.5`（I2C0_SDA）直接连接到MCU的I2C0总线。U1800的I²C设备地址为`0x41`。 * **缺失信息**: * I²C总线上是否还有其他设备（证据显示U1700也连接在同一总线上[3][4]）。 * I²C通信速率、数据格式等详细配置信息。 ### 证据冲突说明 在提供的证据中，存在一些冲突，但这些冲突**不涉及U1800**，主要存在于文档中关于其他芯片（如CAN收发器、一级电源等）的配置描述中。例如，关于`ecu_en`信号的电平（H/L）、`tcan1044a`/`tcan1145`的使用、以及某些`asil`等级的定义等存在不同取值。这些冲突不影响对U1800问题的回答。 ### 总结 | 方面 | 可确认信息 | 缺失信息 | | :--- | :--- | :--- | | **输入引脚** | I²C通信引脚 (SCL, SDA) 连接到MCU。 | 电源输入引脚(VIN)及其他功能引脚连接。 | | **输出检测** | 支持通过I²C进行配置和状态读取。 | 具体检测功能（如电压、电流、温度等）及工作方式。 | | **使能控制** | 由MCU的`MCU_VCC0V75_EN`GPIO信号控制，高电平使能，低电平关断。 | 使能引脚在U1800上的具体连接和电气特性。 | | **I²C控制网络** | 连接到MCU的I2C0总线，设备地址为`0x41`。 | 总线上的其他设备、通信速率等详细配置。 |

When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Table 6-1. ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.
