问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：根据现有证据，对您的问题回答如下： ### 子问题1：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源路径中串联了哪颗负载开关？ **答案：** 该负载开关是 **TPS22918**。 **来源说明：** 文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`中，模块F19的描述明确说明：“采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ，给EQ6供电。”（证据[10]）。同时，在模块方案总览表（证据[9]）中也提到“VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。”并在模块列表（证据[12]）中确认F19模块为“TPS22918 负载开关，VCC1V8电压输入，VCC1V8_EQ电压输出”。 ### 子问题2：该负载开关的使能信号是什么？ **答案：** 使能信号是 **MCU的P20.0引脚**，在文档中也被称为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**。 **来源说明：** 文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`中，模块F19的方案描述（证据[10]）明确：“用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” 在总方案表（证据[9]）中同样描述为“用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” 此外，在模块F19的运行模式描述（证据[15]）中，也提到“MCU通过将MCU_1V8_EQ_EN信号配置为逻辑高使能VCC1V8_EQ电源输出”。 ### 证据冲突说明 在提供的证据冲突列表中，列出了许多不同的值（如`1`, `a14`, `adc`, `p20.3`, `p21.0`等）。经核查，这些冲突值均来源于文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，但它们是该文档中不同模块（如F16、F17、F14等）的配置参数，与您询问的 **VCC1V8到EQ的负载开关（F19模块）** 无关。因此，这些冲突不影响上述结论。 ### 缺失信息 根据检索账本，两个子问题状态均为“covered”（已覆盖），无缺失信息。

问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：好的，已收到您的请求。我将严格基于提供的证据，按子问题组织回答。 ### 文档中声明的CAN相关模块 根据提供的文档证据，板上声明了以下4个CAN相关模块： * **CAN0** (模块序号F23): 基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯 [17] * **CAN1** (模块序号F22): 基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯 [17] * **CAN2** (模块序号F21): 基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯 [17] * **CAN3** (模块序号F20): 基于TCAN1145的CAN通讯，硬件支持特定报文唤醒 [17] **来源说明**：上述信息来源于文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的模块列表 [17] 和模块功能描述 [18]。 ### 网表中CAN模块的具体连接 根据网表文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`，可以确认每个CAN模块的主要网络连接如下。请注意，网表提供了连接的具体节点，但未提供完整的原理图逻辑解释，因此仅列出已观察到的连接。 **CAN0 模块相关网络连接：** * **CAN0H (CAN High 总线)**: 连接至 C1201.&1, D1200.cathode1, L1200.&4, R1209.&2, R1214.&2, TP1206.&1, X1900.&2 [1] * **CAN0L (CAN Low 总线)**: 连接至 C1200.&1, D1200.cathode2, L1200.&3, R1212.&2, R1215.&2, TP1215.&1, X1900.&3 [2] * **CAN0_INH (抑制/使能)**: 连接至 D1203.&1, R1630.&1, TP1219.&1, U1200.INH, U1203.INH [3] * **CAN0_INH_ACQ (抑制信号采集)**: 连接至 C1216.&1, R1236.&2, R1237.&2, TP1232.&1, U900A.AN20/EVADC_G2CH4/EDSADC_EDS2PA [4] * **CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8 (待机/SPI片选)**: 连接至 R1126.&1, R1228.&2, R1241.&1, TP1217.&1, U1200.SCSN, U1202.STB, U1203.NSTB [5] **CAN1 模块相关网络连接：** * **CAN1H (CAN High 总线)**: 连接至 C1207.&1, D1201.cathode1, L1201.&4, R1220.&2, R1224.&2, TP1222.&1, X1900.&10 [6] * **CAN1L (CAN Low 总线)**: 连接至 C1206.&1, D1201.cathode2, L1201.&3, R1223.&2, R1225.&2, TP1223.&1, X1900.&11 [7] * **CAN1_INH (抑制/使能)**: 连接至 D1204.&1, R1629.&1, TP1228.&1, U1204.INH, U1205.INH [8] **CAN2 模块相关网络连接：** * **CAN2H (CAN High 总线)**: 连接至 C1301.&1, D1300.cathode1, L1300.&4, R1302.&2, R1306.&2, TP1301.&1, X1900.&4 [9] * **CAN2L (CAN Low 总线)**: 连接至 C1300.&1, D1300.cathode2, L1300.&3, R1305.&2, R1307.&2, TP1303.&1, X1900.&12 [10] * **CAN2_INH (抑制/使能)**: 连接至 D1303.&1, R1628.&1, TP1314.&1, U1302.INH, U1303.INH [11] * **CAN2_INH_ACQ (抑制信号采集)**: 连接至 C1314.&1, R1329.&2, R1330.&2, TP1321.&1, U900A.AN22/EVADC_G2CH6 [12] * **CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5 (待机/SPI片选)**: 连接至 R1301.&1, R1320.&2, TP1304.&1, U1300.STB, U1302.SCSN, U1303.NSTB, U900D.P00.5/... [13] **CAN3 模块相关网络连接：** * **CAN3H (CAN High 总线)**: 连接至 C1306.&1, D1301.cathode1, L1301.&4, R1311.&2, R1315.&2, TP1307.&1, X1900.&16 [14] * **CAN3L (CAN Low 总线)**: 连接至 C1305.&1, D1301.cathode2, L1301.&3, R1314.&2, R1316.&2, TP1309.&1, X1900.&15 [15] * **CAN3_INH (抑制/使能)**: 连接至 D1304.&1, R1609.&1, TP1319.&1, U1304.INH, U1305.INH [16] **来源说明**：上述连接信息来源于网表文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中对应的网络（Net）定义 [1]-[16]。请注意，对于CAN1和CAN3模块，网表中未提供如 `CAN1_STB` 或 `CAN3_STB` 等网络的具体连接信息。 ### 缺失信息 在上述网表连接查询中，存在以下缺失信息： * 未找到与CAN1模块相关的待机（STB）或SPI片选（CS）信号网络（如 `CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2` 在文档 [18] 中提及）的网表连接记录。 * 未找到与CAN3模块相关的待机（STB）或SPI片选（CS）信号网络（如 `CAN3_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS6_P00.8` 在文档 [18] 中提及）的网表连接记录。 **结论部分**：所有提供的证据均无冲突。

CAN0芯片用到引脚有MCU_CAN0_TXD_P13.0，MCU_CAN0_RXD_P13.1，MCU_SPI3_MTSR_P02.6，CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5，CAN_ERR_MCU_SPI3_CLK_P02.7，CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8。 CAN3芯片用到的引脚有MCU_CAN3_TXD_P32.3，MCU_CAN3_RXD_P32.2，MCU_SPI3_MTSR_P02.6，CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5，CAN_ERR_MCU_SPI3_CLK_P02.7，CAN3_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS6_P00.8。 1, Per system requirements, all four CAN/CAN FD channels in the ADAS must be compatible with both TCAN1145 and TCAN1044A transceivers, supporting a maximum rate of 5 Mbps. The selection of components (TCAN1145 or TCAN1044A) is determined by customer requirements. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, now choose LP87702 PMIC scheme. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，其中VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1输出电压和使能。用MCU的P12.1引脚复位该芯片。 LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50, in which VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR and FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6 and DDR power supply, and VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supplyP15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1 output voltage and enable it. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F02/F05/F10</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>对EQ6和LPDDR4周边PCB温度进行监控。AOU需求MCU监控EQ6芯片内结温，确保EQ6工作安全。AOU需求EQ6监控CAM的工作温度，要求温度监控功能达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统的功能安全。 The PCB temperature around EQ6 and LPDDR4 was monitored. AOU requires MCU to monitor the junction temperature inside EQ6 chip to ensure the safety of EQ6. AOU requires EQ6 to monitor the operating temperature of the CAM, and the temperature monitoring function is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the functional safety of the EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6温度采样 EQ6-L内部内置了两个温度传感器，并通过外部SPI接口实时发送给MCU，硬件设计可以支持MCU对EQ6结温的监控。 1, EQ6 temperature sampling EQ6-l has two internal temperature sensors, which are sent to the MCU in real time through the external SPI interface. The hardware design can support the MCU to monitor the junction temperature of EQ6. NTC温度采样 硬件设计使用温敏电阻NTC（NCU15XH103F6SRC）采集EQ6/LPDDR4周边环境温度，并通过MCU进行ADC采样和实时监控。NTC温度监控范围：-40~150℃，监控精度±3℃@-40~150℃，MCU的ADC为10bit，即1024个量阶，使得监测精度可以达到3.2mV，电压采集范围0-3.3V。用MCU的AN7引脚采样。 2.NTC temperature sampling In hardware design, the temperature sensor NTC (NCU15XH103F6SRC) is used to collect the ambient temperature of EQ6/LPDDR4, and the ADC sampling and real-time monitoring are carried out by MCU. NTC temperature monitoring range: -40-150℃, monitoring accuracy ±3℃@-40-150℃, MCU ADC is 10bit, that is, 1024 quantity order, so that the monitoring accuracy can reach 3.2mV, voltage acquisition range 0-3.3V. The AN7 pin of the MCU is used for sampling. CAM温度采样 CAM的IMX-728内置了两个温度传感器，温度测量范围是：-40~125℃，温度监控精度为±5℃@-40~125℃。EYEQ6可以通过I2C接口（EQ6L的I2C_0_SCLK和I2C_0_SDA引脚）实时读取IMX-728的工作温度，并进行状态监控和异常诊断。 3.CAM temperature sampling CAM IMX-728 built-in two temperature sensors, temperature measurement range is: -40~125℃, temperature monitoring accuracy of ±5℃@-40~125℃. EYEQ6 can read the operating temperature of the IMX-728 in real time through the I2C interface (I2C_0_SCLK and I2C_0_SDA pins of EQ6L), and perform condition monitoring and anomaly diagnosis. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | Updated theDevice OptionsTable. 3 .5 Modified MODE/SYNC pin Description in Pin Functions Changed the title of Figure 8-5 12 Added approximate minimum on time value. 15 Added behavior of device when EN is toggled and input voltage is applied.. .17 · Modified Table 9-5 to reflect new available Orderable Part Number.. 21 Added a comment on how to connect secondary devices SYNC_OUT pin.. 27 Added a footnote to Table 9-10 .31 Added I2C Register Reset .34 Added Number of phases NΦ and adapted passive component selection equations in Typical Application ...41 Page Changes from Revision * (May 2022) to Revision A (July 2022) Changed document status from Advance Information to Production Data...... .1 5 Description (continued) The switching frequency is resistor-selectable through the FSEL pin. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. 问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：根据现有证据，VCC3V3 网络在板上主要给以下电源器件供电（这些器件以 VCC3V3 为输入，输出其他电压或为下游模块供电）： | 电源器件 | 输入网络 | 输出网络/功能 | 证据来源 | |----------|----------|----------------|----------| | LP87702 PMIC (F13) | VCC3V3 | VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50 | 文档[18] F13 行、文档[19] “VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50” | | TPS62872 DC-DC (F14) | VCC3V3 | VCC0V75 | 文档[18] F14 行、文档[19] “VCC3V3 to VCC0V75” | | SA23002 LDO/DC-DC (F15) | VCC3V3 | VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR | 文档[18] F15 行、文档[19] “VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR” | | TPS22918 负载开关 (U1802, F16) | VCC3V3 | VCC3V3_EQ | 电路[4] (U1802.VIN 连 VCC3V3, U1802.VOUT 连 VCC3V3_EQ)、文档[18] F16 行 | | TPS22918 负载开关 (U1500, F17) | VCC3V3 | VCC3V3_ETH | 电路[5] (U1500.VIN 连 VCC3V3, U1500.VOUT 连 VCC3V3_ETH)、文档[18] F17 行 | | MCU 内置 BUCK 控制器 (F27) | VCC3V3_MCU（来自 VCC3V3） | VCC1V25_MCU | 文档[18] F27 行、文档[19] “VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU” | **说明**： - 以上器件均**以 VCC3V3 或其分支（如 VCC3V3_MCU、VCC3V3_EQ）作为输入**，属于电源转换或开关类器件，而非电阻、电容等无源负载。 - 证据中未明确由 VCC3V3 直接供电的其他电源器件： - U1501（TPS74501，LDO），其输入为 VCC3V3_ETH，但证据未给出其输出网络，无法确认是否为电源负载。 - U901（FDS8958A，MOSFET），其 S2 连接 VCC3V3_SW，但功能未明确（可能为开关，但无输出网络说明）。 - U1600、U1700、U1800、U1804 等芯片的 VIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3，但缺少型号与功能描述，无法判断是否为电源器件。 **缺失信息**： - 子问题 **sq_2（这些器件中哪些是电源器件）** 在检索账本中状态为 **missing**，缺少直接证据（如器件型号、功能描述）来确认所有连接 VCC3V3 的器件哪些属于电源转换类。当前列表仅基于文档中明确为“POWER”模块的器件，其他未覆盖的器件（如 U1501、U901 等）需补充 datasheet 或模块说明才能确认。 **证据冲突**： 检索结果中存在若干字段冲突（如 `1v25`、`3v3`、`a19` 等值在同一文档中多次出现），但未提供具体上下文，无法确定这些冲突是否影响对电源器件的判断。当前回答未使用冲突字段，因此不影响上述结论。

Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F12</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，基于LN10046设计，给系统提供电源。 The KL30 to VCC3V3 is a first-level DC-DC based on the LN10046 design and provides power to the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用LN10046电源DCDC芯片，将12V系统电源转换为3.3V，提供给系统供电使用。 The LN10046 power supply DCDC chip is used to convert the 12V system power supply to 3.3V and provide power supply to the system for use. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | MCU Component ：F01/MCU; NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F15</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR应用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用SA23002电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为0V75_MIPI_DDR，提供给EQ6的IO口供电。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 SA23002 power chip is used to convert VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR to supply power to the I/O port of EQ6. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | 问题：从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。
参考答案：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 使能 U1500，VCC3V3 经 U1500 形成 VCC3V3_ETH，再进入 U1501 TPS74501，输出 VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，无法完整追踪从MCU使能到以太网PHY 1.0V电源的完整路径。以下按子问题逐一说明现有证据、来源以及缺失信息。 ### 1. MCU的使能信号是哪个引脚或信号？ **现有证据**： - 电路设计（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中列出了多个MCU输出的使能信号，如`CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8`、`CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2`、`CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5`、`CAN3_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS6_P00.8`、`CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5`、`CAN_ERR_MCU_SPI3_CLK_P02.7`、`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`等。 - 这些信号分别连接至CAN收发器或均衡器（EQ）的使能/控制引脚（如`U1202.STB`、`U1802.ON`等）。 **缺失信息**： - **没有明确标识用于控制以太网PHY 1.0V电源的使能信号**。上述使能信号均与CAN、均衡器或其它模块相关，未出现例如`PHY_1V0_EN`、`ETH_1V0_EN`等与以太网PHY 1.0V电源直接关联的MCU引脚或网络。 - 尽管检索账本将该子问题标记为“covered”，但实际可用的电路证据中缺乏与以太网PHY 1.0V电源使能相关的直接连接。 ### 2. 该使能信号如何控制1.0V电源？中间有哪些电源管理元件（如LDO、开关）？ **现有证据**： - 文档证据（来源：600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）的片段（ID: 7a6ae2179c8765b3, 72947b599dcff97a等）被标记为支持，但**未提供具体文本内容**，无法获取其中关于电源管理元件（LDO、开关）的详细描述。 - 电路设计证据中仅观察到`VCC1V0_ETH`网络（连接至`U1501.OUT`、`L1403.&2`、`C1420.&1`等），表明`U1501`可能是产生1.0V电源的器件，但**未提供U1501的型号、使能引脚或控制逻辑**。 - 其它电路证据（如`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`）涉及不同电源（1.8V、3.3V），与1.0V电源无关。 **缺失信息**： - **缺少使能信号到1.0V电源之间电源管理元件的具体信息**：没有证据表明上述任何MCU使能信号直接或间接控制`U1501`或其它产生1.0V的器件。 - **文档证据内容缺失**：无法从已提供的文档片段中提取任何关于LDO/开关的使能关系、引脚连接等关键信息。 - **电路设计证据中未发现中间元件（如LDO、开关）的使能网络**。 ### 3. 从使能信号到以太网PHY 1.0V电源的完整路径是什么？ **现有证据**： - 电路设计证据中列出了`PHY_MDIN`、`PHY_MDIP`、`PHY_RESET_N_P22.3`、`PHY_WAKE_P21.5`等网络，这些是PHY的数据差分信号、复位和唤醒信号，**与电源路径无关**。 - `VCC1V0_ETH`网络被识别为以太网PHY的1.0V电源网络（连接到`L1403.&2`、`U1501.OUT`等），但**未展示该网络与任何MCU使能信号之间的连接**。 - 模块连接（`Group 14 · Ethernet/PHY:B_D_ETH_1000_N`、`B_D_ETH_1000_P`）仅涉及差分数据线，不涉及电源。 **缺失信息**： - **无法确定使能信号到1.0V电源的完整路径**：缺少使能信号（如某个MCU GPIO）到电源管理元件（如LDO U1501）的使能引脚之间的网络连接。 - **没有证据表明任何使能信号直接控制VCC1V0_ETH的生成或开关**。 - 路径中的关键环节（如电源芯片的使能输入、输出滤波、供电使能开关等）均未在证据中体现。 ### 证据冲突 根据用户提供的冲突列表，存在以下字段取值不一致（均来自同一文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）： - 字段“none”的取值包括：`1`, `a18`, `a4`, `b`, `b15`, `b17`, `cfg1`, `cfg2`, `config`, `d6`, `d7`, `eq6l`, `eq6l_gpio_a0_p23.0`, `eq6l_gpio_a1_p23.1`, `eq_cfg`, `esr0`, `esr1`, `eyeq6_ncrerr_n_p15.1`, `eyeq6l`, `f13`, `f14`, `f16`, `f17`, `flash`, `g16`, `g20`, `gpio`, `hi-z`, `i`, `k17`, `mcu_3v3_eq_en`, `mcu_3v3_eth_en`, `mcu_5v_en`, `mcu_eth_cfg1_p11.14`, `mcu_eth_cfg2_p11.15`, `mcu_rst_flash_p23.2`, `mcu_vcc0v75_en`, `na`, `nmi`, `o`, `p10.6`, `p11.14`, `p11.15`, `p14.4`, `p15.1`, `p15.3`, `p20`, `p20.3`, `p21.0`, `p22.0`, `p23.0`, `p23.1`, `p23.2`, `pms_hwcfg5in`, `pms_hwcfg6in`, `qm`, `u19`, `u20`, `v20`, `vcc0v75`, `vcc3v3_eq`, `vcc3v3_eth`, `vcc50`, `warmreset`。 **原因**：不同证据对同一字段给出了不同取值。 - 字段“gpioxx gpioyy”的取值包括：`00`, `01`, `10`, `11`。 **原因**：不同证据对同一字段给出了不同取值。 这些冲突来源于同一文档，可能涉及引脚配置或寄存器值，但由于缺乏具体上下文，无法判断其与以太网PHY 1.0V电源路径的相关性。**无法合并为确定结论**，需视为未知冲突。 ### 总结 **当前证据无法完整回答用户问题**。主要缺口包括： - 未明确用于控制以太网PHY 1.0V电源的MCU使能信号。 - 缺少使能信号到1.0V电源之间电源管理元件（LDO、开关）的详细连接和使能逻辑。 - 文档证据内容未提供，电路设计证据中无相关网络连接。 - 存在多处证据冲突，无法确认具体配置。 建议补充以下信息： - 提供以太网PHY 1.0V电源产生器件（如U1501）的型号、使能引脚及控制信号。 - 提供MCU与电源管理元件之间的使能网络连接（如`PHY_1V0_EN`等）。 - 提供相关文档中关于电源使能控制的完整文本。

Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC. System requirements require power management for the product power supply KL30. The hardware circuit performs ADC sampling on the UBD power supply.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号是由MCU I/O控制的数字信号，用于使能KL30电压采样功能。 MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal is a digital signal controlled by MCU I/O, used to enable KL30 voltage sampling function. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MCU的ADC采集到UBD后进行电源管理，当软件诊断到UBD电压范围异常时，MCU会关断EQ和其他负载工作电源，确保ADAS功能安全。 The ADC of MCU collects UBD for power management. When the software detects that UBD voltage range is abnormal, the MCU will turn off EQ and other load power supplies to ensure the functional safety of ADAS.</td></tr></table> | None：EYEQ6L 非关键性错误指示信号; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：A18; None：P15.1; None：P15.1/GTM_TIM3_IN4_4/GTM_TIM2_IN4_4/CAN02_RXDA/QSPI2_SLSIB/GTM_TOUT72/QSPI2_SLSO5; None：GPIO; None：EYEQ6_NCRERR_N_P15.1; None：/; None：I; None：EYEQ6L 非关键性错误指示信号 高：未发生错误 低：发生错误; None：HI-Z, 硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：1、EQ6L的GPIOxx、GPIOyy信号，具体功能如下所示： GPIOxx GPIOyy=00：Pass GPIOxx GPIOyy=10：Boot failed due to high temperature GPIOxx GPIOyy=01：Boot failed due to one of the BISTs failing GPIOxx GPIOyy=11：N/A - invalid GPIO value ; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：V20; None：P23.0; None：P23.0/GTM_TIM1_IN5_4/GTM_TIM0_IN5_4/CAN10_RXDC/GTM_TOUT41; None：EQ_CFG; None：EQ6L_GPIO_A0_P23.0; None：/; None：O; None：EQ6L的GPIOxx(HIGH_TEMPER_GPIO) 状态信号，EQ的事件指示引脚，配合GPIOyy使用。 ; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：U19; None：P23.1; None：P23.1/GTM_TIM1_IN6_4/GTM_TIM0_IN6_4/MSC1_SDI0/GTM_TOUT42/QSPI4_SLSO6/GTM_CLK0/CAN10_TXD; None：EQ_CFG; None：EQ6L_GPIO_A1_P23.1; None：/; None：O; None：EQ6L的GPIOxx(HIGH_TEMPER_GPIO) 状态信号，EQ的事件指示引脚，配合GPIOyy使用。 ; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1
